# Step 5 — Moral Duplications (2026-09-25)

Scope: the entire repository (83 `.lean` files) — first Step 5 to include `PhD/Modularforms/` and `PhD/ModularFunctions/`. Paths are relative to `/Users/dfa25dxu/Projects/Lean/XYin/`; line numbers are from the working tree on branch `PhD_Experiment` (oleans of 2026-09-21, build green). Mathlib paths are under `.lake/packages/mathlib/` (pin `2abc242`).

## 1. Method

Seven cluster finders (analytic/Modularforms, Laurent–j–integral series, Cox matrices/HNF/lattices, modular polynomial, Kronecker/LevelThree, ModularFunctions/Cox fields, and a July-2026 table re-check) read the fresh per-file inventories and opened every candidate pair in source before rating it (statement identity, proof identity, verdict, one-line action, lines saved). The 45 rows that were deletion-grade or capped (the ones whose action deletes or rewrites declarations) were then adversarially verified by independent agents against the current source and, where a claim mattered, by `lake env lean` scratch compiles; 34 were confirmed (often with implementer caveats, recorded in §7) and 11 were refuted or corrected — those keep their corrected verdict below with the refutation note in the Action column. The 35 rows of the July 2026 duplication table were re-checked one by one against the post-cleanup source (§3).

Verdict legend: DELETE-ONE (one copy is dead or a pure alias), REPLACE-WITH-MATHLIB (mathlib has it, grep-verified path given), UNIFY (one parametric lemma replaces both), DERIVE (keep both names, reprove one from the other), PUBLICIZE (drop `private`/move upstream so the re-proof disappears), EXTRACT-HELPER (a shared lemma removes the copied block), DISTINCT (not a duplicate; keep). "Lines saved" is the finder's estimate. "Verified?" = confirmed [V-n] (note in §7) / refuted → corrected verdict / no.

## 2. Pairwise table

214 rows in seven cluster tables (rows numbered globally). Within each cluster the deletion-grade verdicts (DELETE-ONE, REPLACE-WITH-MATHLIB, UNIFY) come first, then DERIVE / PUBLICIZE / EXTRACT-HELPER, then DISTINCT; verified rows first within a verdict. The July re-check table is ordered by July row number and overlaps with the cluster tables (its savings are not additive).

```lean
lemma bound_of_mem_verticalStrip (s : ℍ) {A B : ℝ} (hB : 0 < B) (hs : s ∈ verticalStrip A B) (k : ℕ) (n : ℕ+) (ε : ℤ) (hε : ε.natAbs = 1) : ‖((-1 : ℂ) ^ (k + 1) * (k + 1)! * (1 / (s + ε * n) ^ (k + 2)))‖ ≤ ‖((k + 1)! / r ⟨⟨A, B⟩, by simp [hB]⟩ ^ (k + 2)) * ((n : ℝ) ^ ((k : ℤ) + 2))⁻¹‖
```

```lean
lemma UpperHalfPlane.periodic_comp_ofComplex_of_vadd {F : ℍ → ℂ} {c : ℝ} (hF : ∀ τ : ℍ, F (c +ᵥ τ) = F τ) : Periodic (F ∘ UpperHalfPlane.ofComplex) c := by intro w; by_cases hw : 0 < Complex.im w; · have hw1 : 0 < Complex.im (w + c) := by simpa using hw; have hτ : (c +ᵥ (⟨w, hw⟩ : ℍ)) = ofComplex (w + c) := by apply UpperHalfPlane.ext; simp [ofComplex_apply_of_im_pos hw1, coe_vadd, add_comm]; simpa [Function.comp, hτ, ofComplex_apply_of_im_pos hw] using hF ⟨w, hw⟩; · have hw0 := le_of_not_gt hw; have hw1 : Complex.im (w + c) ≤ 0 := by simpa using hw0; simp [Function.comp, ofComplex_apply_of_im_nonpos hw1, ofComplex_apply_of_im_nonpos hw0]  (scratch-verified; upstream candidate — mathlib's SlashInvariantFormClass.periodic_comp_ofComplex QExpansion.lean:120–131 is the same skeleton for forms only)
```

noncomputable def LaurentSeries.substMonomial {K : Type*} [Field K] (c : K) (r : ℕ) (hr : r ≠ 0) (F : LaurentSeries K) : LaurentSeries K -- coeff_substMonomial : (substMonomial c r hr F).coeff k = if (r : ℤ) ∣ k then c ^ (k / r) * F.coeff (k / r) else 0; theorem laurentqExpansion_comp_of_qParam_eq {h h' : ℝ} (hh : 0 < h) {φ : ℍ → ℍ} {c : ℂ} (hc : c ≠ 0) {r : ℕ} (hr : r ≠ 0) (hq : ∀ τ, Periodic.qParam h' (φ τ) = c * Periodic.qParam h τ ^ r) {f : ℍ → ℂ} (hmero : MeromorphicAt (cuspFunction h' f) 0) (hper : Periodic (f ∘ UpperHalfPlane.ofComplex) h') : laurentqExpansion h (f ∘ φ) = LaurentSeries.substMonomial c r hr (laurentqExpansion h' f)

namespace Matrix.IsColumnHermiteNormalForm
variable {A : Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) ℤ} (hA : A.IsColumnHermiteNormalForm)
theorem one_zero : A 1 0 = 0
theorem zero_zero_pos : 0 < A 0 0
theorem one_one_pos : 0 < A 1 1
theorem zero_one_mem : A 0 1 ∈ Set.Ico 0 (A 0 0)
theorem eq_fin_two : A = !![A 0 0, A 0 1; 0, A 1 1]
theorem det_fin_two : A.det = A 0 0 * A 1 1
theorem rankTwoContent_eq :
    A.rankTwoContent = Nat.gcd (Nat.gcd (A 1 1).natAbs (A 0 1).natAbs) (A 0 0).natAbs

```lean
theorem Matrix.toMatrix_rangeBasis_subtype {K M : Type*} [AddCommGroup K] [AddCommGroup M] {n : ℕ}
    (bK : Module.Basis (Fin n) ℤ K) (bM : Module.Basis (Fin n) ℤ M) (A : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) ℤ)
    (hf : Function.Injective (Matrix.toLin bK bM A)) :
    LinearMap.toMatrix (bK.map (LinearEquiv.ofInjective _ hf)) bM
      (LinearMap.range (Matrix.toLin bK bM A)).subtype = A
def Module.Basis.HermiteNormalForm.ofToLin {K M : Type*} [AddCommGroup K] [AddCommGroup M] {n : ℕ}
    (bK : Module.Basis (Fin n) ℤ K) (bM : Module.Basis (Fin n) ℤ M) {A : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) ℤ}
    (hA : A.IsColumnHermiteNormalForm) (hf : Function.Injective (Matrix.toLin bK bM A)) :
    HermiteNormalForm bM (LinearMap.range (Matrix.toLin bK bM A)) :=
  ⟨bK.map (LinearEquiv.ofInjective _ hf), A, Matrix.toMatrix_rangeBasis_subtype bK bM A hf, hA⟩
theorem Matrix.toLin_injective_of_det_ne_zero {K M : Type*} [AddCommGroup K] [AddCommGroup M] {n : ℕ}
    (bK : Module.Basis (Fin n) ℤ K) (bM : Module.Basis (Fin n) ℤ M) {A : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) ℤ}
    (hA : A.det ≠ 0) : Function.Injective (Matrix.toLin bK bM A)
-- via Matrix.exists_mulVec_eq_zero_iff (Mathlib/LinearAlgebra/Matrix/ToLinearEquiv.lean:161).
```

**H40. `CoxMatrix.coe_act_eq_of_sl2_mul_eq`**

- Home: XYin/PhD/Cox/CoxMatrices/Basic.lean

```lean
theorem CoxMatrix.coe_act_eq_of_sl2_mul_eq {N : ℕ+} {σ : CoxMatrix N} {γ : SL(2, ℤ)} {M : Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) ℤ} (hmat : γ.1 * M = σ.mobiusMatrix) (τ : ℍ) (hM : ((M 1 0 : ℂ) * τ + M 1 1) ≠ 0) : ((σ.act τ : ℍ) : ℂ) = ((γ 0 0 : ℂ) * w + γ 0 1) / ((γ 1 0 : ℂ) * w + γ 1 1) where w := ((M 0 0 : ℂ) * τ + M 0 1) / ((M 1 0 : ℂ) * τ + M 1 1)  (γ-denominator nonzero follows from (γ·M) 1 0 = 0, (γ·M) 1 1 = σ.d ≠ 0)
```

```

```

## 7. Verification notes for confirmed rows

Full adversarial-verification text for the 34 confirmed rows (refuted rows carry their note inline in §2). These contain the implementer caveats (extra callers, cast bridges, ordering constraints) that the one-line actions omit.

- **[V-1] row 1 — hasDerivAt_tsum_fun (XYin/PhD/Modularforms/tsumderivWithin.lean:36):** Hypotheses at XYin/PhD/Modularforms/tsumderivWithin.lean:36-41, :57-61, :66-72 are byte-identical (only binder sugar differs) and B1/B2 are one-line projections of A (:63-64, :74); grep -rnw over all .lean outside .lake finds zero callers for all three, so deleting derivWithin_tsum_fun' and hasDerivWithinAt_tsum_fun breaks nothing, and B1 is verbatim derivable from mathlib derivWithin_tsum (Mathlib/Topology/Algebra/InfiniteSum/TsumUniformlyOn.lean:72) + SummableLocallyUniformlyOn_of_locally_bounded (:56) (scratch compiled, standard axioms). Caveat for the orchestrator: the "all three subsumed by mathlib" side-claim over-reaches for A — mathlib exposes no HasDerivAt/HasDerivWithinAt form under local bounds (derivWithin_tsum's proof :77-86 is A's proof plus B1's step but discards the HasDerivAt; SmoothSeries.lean:90/134 need a global majorant) — yet A is dead too after the deletion since the sole importer Summability/QExpansion.lean:183 already uses mathlib iteratedDerivWithin_tsum, so the whole triple can go in one sweep.
- **[V-2] row 2 — E4_qExpansion_coeff_aux (XYin/PhD/jFunction/Deltaqexpansions.lean:41):** Confirmed: Deltaqexpansions.lean:36 `abbrev E4 := _root_.E₄` is exactly the `def E₄` of Eisenstein.lean:53 (root namespace, `@[expose] public section` :19, no `namespace` in file), so `E4_qExpansion_coeff_aux` (:41) / `E6_qExpansion_coeff_aux` (:47) are the pointwise forms of public `E4_q_exp` (Eisenstein.lean:265) / `E6_q_exp` (:294); a scratch compile with Deltaqexpansions' exact imports/opens proves both aux statements by `congr_fun E4_q_exp n` / `congr_fun E6_q_exp n` and re-runs all 4 call-site shapes each (:58,:59,:115,:131 / :66,:67,:119,:137) cleanly (lake env lean exit 0). Mathlib has only the Bernoulli-form `EisensteinSeries.E_qExpansion_coeff` (QExpansion.lean:324) and coeff-0/1 specialisations, so no REPLACE-WITH-MATHLIB; keep the private names as 1-line aliases (or inline) and delete the duplicated proofs.
- **[V-3] row 3 — ResToImagAxis (XYin/PhD/Modularforms/ResToImagAxis.lean:32):** Confirmed: XYin/PhD/Modularforms/ResToImagAxis.lean:38 is `noncomputable def resToImagAxis (F : ℍ → ℂ) : ℝ → ℂ := ResToImagAxis F` (same signature, no hypotheses, both in the `@[expose] public section` at :20), with :41/:44 `rfl` in a green build; no `open/namespace ResToImagAxis` exists, so the dotted names ResToImagAxis.Real/.Pos/... (:53/:60, not :52/:59) survive. Correction to the action: the 3 bare uses (:38, :41, :44) are the deleted lines themselves, but `ResToImagAxis` is also an unfold target in 18 simp sets (own file :84,:96,:102,:106,:110,:118,:130,:164,:253,:255,:282,:388,:394,:402; Delta.lean :331,:370; Eisenstein.lean :474,:542) plus :74 `rw [Function.resToImagAxis_eq_resToImagAxis]` and :118 `resToImagAxis_apply` reference the deleted rfl lemmas — all 20 edits are mechanical (drop the name from the simp list / drop the rw), verified by a scratch compile of the inlined definition with the reduced simp sets.
- **[V-4] row 6 — pnat_inv_sub_squares (XYin/PhD/Modularforms/Summability/Reciprocal.lean:33):** Not refuted: a scratch compile (lake env lean, exit 0, single import of Cotangent.lean) re-proved A's verbatim statement (XYin/PhD/Modularforms/Summability/Reciprocal.lean:33-42, z : ℍ, ℕ+-indexed funext, 1/(z²-n²)) from mathlib's public root-level `cotTerm_identity (UpperHalfPlane.coe_mem_integerComplement z) k` (.lake/packages/mathlib/Mathlib/Analysis/SpecialFunctions/Trigonometric/Cotangent.lean:203; IntegerCompl.lean:37) via `Nat.exists_eq_add_one_of_ne_zero n.ne_zero` + `rw [sq_sub_sq, hn]`, and the finder's replacement term type-checks as written. Zero callers repo-wide (grep -rnw: only the definition; _crossfile-usage.tsv:1417 = 0/0; the lone historical caller is a removed `-` line in .mathlib-quality/proof-cleanup/full-cleanup.diff:7423) and Cotangent.lean is already in Reciprocal's import closure (Reciprocal.lean:64 uses `summable_cotTerm (coe_mem_integerComplement z)`), so deleting lines 33-42 breaks nothing and needs no new import.
